# Spec checklist

Everything the specs describe, generated by `scripts/spec_list.py`. Tick a box by changing `[ ]` to `[x]`; a rerun keeps your ticks as long as the item's text is unchanged. Tags in `{ }` are evidence from text matching, not a sign-off: "named in code or notes" means the section's title appears there, and many built sections use other wording, so a section without the tag may still be built.

This covers what the spec files state, down to their sections and level-script lines. Numbers inside a spec's tables are part of their section, not separate items.
